[REASONING]
...

Since 3 out of 4 neighbors are cooperating, I will receive a benefit of b = 3. If I cooperate, I will pay a cost of c for each of my 4 neighbors, which is 4c. My reward would be 3b - 4c = 9 - 4c. If I defect, I will receive no benefit and pay no cost, so my reward would be 0.

Since my reward for cooperating (9 - 4c) is greater than my reward for defecting (0), I should cooperate to maximize my reward.
